# List of modern equipment of the German Army

This page contains a list of equipment currently in service with the [German Army](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/German_Army). Concerning equipment that has recently been adopted or is in the process of being adopted, this list here somewhat overlaps with the list in the article [Future equipment of the German Army](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Future_equipment_of_the_German_Army). The article "Future equipment of the German Army" contains a list of equipment on order or planned to be ordered, which hasn't yet entered service in the German Army.

## Weapons

| [Heckler & Koch USP](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heckler_&_Koch_P8) *Heckler & Koch P8A1* |  | [Germany](11867.md) | [Semi-automatic pistol](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Semi-automatic_pistol) | [9×19mm Parabellum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/9%C3%9719mm_Parabellum) | [^cite_note-3] Current standard [service pistol](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Service_pistol) of the [Bundeswehr](288188.md). In the meantime, the [pistol](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pistol) has been upgraded to the P8A1 version with a reinforced breech. Another variant is the [P8](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/P8_Pistol) [Combat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Combat), which differs from the [P8](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/P8_Pistol) in the lack of a safety lever. The P8 replaced the [Walther P1](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Walther_P38), [Colt M1911A1](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Colt_M1911A1), [SIG P210-4](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SIG_P210) and the [Astra 600](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Astra_600)/43 as the [soldiers'](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Soldier) standard [pistol](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pistol). To be replaced by the P13. |

| Assault rifles / battle rifles |  |  |  |  |  |
| [Heckler & Koch G36](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heckler_&_Koch_G36) (multiple variants) |  | [Germany](11867.md) | [Assault rifle](18950865.md) / [carabine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carbine) | [5.56×45mm NATO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/5.56%C3%9745mm_NATO) | [^cite_note-17] **[Service rifle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Service_rifle)** that replaced the [HK G3](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heckler_&_Koch_G3). The weapons were delivered to the Bundeswehr from 1996 to 2014,[^cite_note-18] with an expected service life of 20 years. In 2015, 176,544 G36s had been purchased and 166,619 were in use. As of 2019, the versions in use are the A0 to A4 and the shorter variant kA1 to kA4. The variants A3 and kA3 are part of the equipment of the [Infanterist der Zukunft](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Infanterist_der_Zukunft). The variant A4 removes a push-to-talk switch on the weapon. As part of the maintenance, all defective G36s are upgraded to version A4.[^cite_note-ES\&T_Redaktion2-5] The [HK416A8](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heckler_&_Koch_HK416) was selected to replace the G36 in December 2022; the weapon would be designated the [G95](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heckler_&_Koch_G95). |
| [Heckler & Koch G95](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heckler_&_Koch_G95) *G95A1 and G95KA1* | – | [Germany](11867.md) | [Assault rifle](18950865.md) / [carabine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carbine) | [5.56×45mm NATO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/5.56%C3%9745mm_NATO) | **250,000 new** **[standard service rifle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Service_rifle) ordered.** They were selected in December 2022. It entered service in December 2025.[^cite_note-19] Specifications and accessories: Standard barrel likely 16.5 inches (G95A1), for the short barrel variant, likely 14 inches (G95KA1).[^cite_note-ph-20] [5.56×45mm NATO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/5.56%C3%9745mm_NATO) Standard optical sight, the adjustable [ELCAN SpecterDR, 1-4 times](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ELCAN_Optical_Technologies).[^cite_note-21] Laser/light module LLM-VarioRay by Rheinmetall.[^cite_note-22] Funding approvals: December 2022: **118,718** rifles (€209 million)[^cite_note-23] Firm order in January 2023:[^cite_note-24][^cite_note-ph-20] **13,929** G95A1 **3,104** G95KA1 December 2025: framework agreement amended to **250,000** rifles[^cite_note-db-25][^cite_note-26], followed by the order of the entirety of the amendment.[^cite_note-27] Deliveries: May 2025, first deliveries for trials[^cite_note-28] Plan for 250,000 additional to be ordered as of August 2026.[^cite_note-29] |
| [Heckler & Koch HK416 A7](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heckler_&_Koch_HK416) *G95K* | – | [Germany](11867.md) | [Assault rifle](18950865.md) | [5.56×45mm NATO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/5.56%C3%9745mm_NATO) | [^cite_note-30] The [KSK](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kommando_Spezialkr%C3%A4fte) introduced the G95K in service in 2019 as their new standard assault rifle, replacing the HK G36. It was selected after having been tested in 2017. 1,745 weapons were ordered.[^cite_note-31] Accessories: [Eotech](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/EOTech) supplied the [reflector sight](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Reflector_sight) EXPS 3-0 NV and its [red dot magnifier](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_dot_magnifier) G33™ a mission silencer B\&T MARS and a practice silencer are provided[^cite_note-32] [Rheinmetall](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rheinmetall) supplied the LM-VTAL, a tactical laser and light module, and the TL-MISSIONLIGHT the [combat swimmers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Combat_swimmers) received an [Aimpoint sight](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aimpoint_sight) ERATAC BUIS (*back up iron sight*)[^cite_note-33] [B\&T](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Br%C3%BCgger_&_Thomet) mono bloc silencer |
| [Heckler & Koch HK417](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heckler_&_Koch_HK417) *G27P* | *(Illustration)* | [Germany](11867.md) | [Battle rifle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Battle_rifle) | [7.62×51mm NATO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/7.62%C3%9751mm_NATO) | [^cite_note-34] Used by the regular a member of an infantry squad to increase the fire power on the battlefield. 600 were ordered in this variant with the 16'' barrel.[^cite_note-35] |
| [Heckler & Koch HK417 A2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heckler_&_Koch_HK417) *G27K* | *(Illustration)* | [Germany](11867.md) | [Battle rifle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Battle_rifle) | [7.62×51mm NATO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/7.62%C3%9751mm_NATO) | Ordered in December 2010 to be used by the [KSK](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kommando_Spezialkr%C3%A4fte).[^cite_note-ks-12] 157 were ordered with a 13'' barrel (108 for the army, 49 in use with the [Navy KSK](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kommando_Spezialkr%C3%A4fte_Marine)).[^cite_note-36][^cite_note-37] |

| [Accuracy International AWM-F](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/G22_Sniper_Rifle) *G22A2* |  | [United Kingdom](31717.md)  [Germany](11867.md) (A2) | [Bolt action](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bolt_action) [sniper rifle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sniper_rifle) | [7.62×67mmB](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/.300_Winchester_Magnum) | [^cite_note-44] Intended to be used against man targets up to 800 meters and against tactical targets such as [radar systems](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Radar_systems), [helicopters](8286923.md) or unarmoured [vehicles](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vehicle) up to 1000 meters. According to the principles of use, the [G22](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/G22_Sniper_Rifle) is intended for defensive or reactive use ("counter-[sniping](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sniping)"). However, an [offensive](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Offensive_weapon) use of the [weapon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Weapon) is also possible. As of 2019, all 780 [rifles](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rifle) have been upgraded to standard A2.[^cite_note-45][^cite_note-46] |

| [Barrett M82A1](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/G82) *G82A1* |  | [United States](3434750.md) | [Anti materiel sniper rifle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-materiel) | [12.7×99mm NATO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/12.7%C3%9799mm_NATO) | [^cite_note-49] Introduced to the [Bundeswehr](288188.md) as a long-range rifle as part of the [Infantryman of the Future project](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Infanterist_der_Zukunft) and is considered an alternative when the [G36](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heckler_&_Koch_G36) and [G22](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/G22_Sniper_Rifle) reach their performance limits. | Machine guns |  |  |  |  |  |
| [Heckler & Koch 123](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heckler_&_Koch_MG4) *MG4A3* |  | [Germany](11867.md) | [LMG](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Light_machine_gun) | [5.56×45mm NATO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/5.56%C3%9745mm_NATO) | [^cite_note-51] The [MG4](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MG4_machine_gun) was developed and introduced as part of the [Infantryman of the Future project](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Infanterist_der_Zukunft). There are currently five versions for different purposes. These are to be consolidated into three versions in the future, but already have a high level of logistical equality (123 transferable parts) as well as similar usability and the same security concept.[^cite_note-ES\&T_Redaktion2-5] |
| [Rheinmetall MG3](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rheinmetall_MG3) |  | [West Germany](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/West_Germany) | [GPMG](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/General-purpose_machine_gun) | [7.62×51mm NATO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/7.62%C3%9751mm_NATO) | [^cite_note-52] The [MG3](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MG_3_machine_gun) is a further development of the [MG42](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MG_42) from the [Second World War](32927.md) and is still used in the [Bundeswehr](288188.md) today. Since production already ended in the 1970s, it is currently planned to have several thousand new cases milled. As of 2019, it remains in use primarily on [combat vehicles](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Combat_vehicle) as an [anti-aircraft gun](146640.md), but also as a [machine gun](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Machine_gun).[^cite_note-ES\&T_Redaktion2-5] |
| [Heckler & Koch 121](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heckler_&_Koch_MG5) *MG5A2* |  | [Germany](11867.md) | [GPMG](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/General-purpose_machine_gun) | [7.62×51mm NATO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/7.62%C3%9751mm_NATO) | [^cite_note-53] Intended to replace [MG3](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MG_3_machine_gun). The MG5A1 can be integrated into the FLW100 [remote-controlled weapon stations](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Remote_controlled_weapon_station) and the KMW1530 mechanical mounts. Adapters are also to be introduced for the MG5A2 so that it can be used on [MG3](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MG_3_machine_gun) mounts.[^cite_note-ES\&T_Redaktion2-5][^cite_note-54] The 15,000th rifle was delivered to the Bundeswehr by May 2025.[^cite_note-55] |

From the A1 variant onwards, the machine gun has a stepped piston for better grip and control. It is made for temperate climates, typically western / north-west Europe.[^cite_note-114][^cite_note-three-color_camouflage_print-115] The colours of the camoulage are:[^cite_note-116][^cite_note-117] light green (15%) light olive (20%) dark green (35%) brown (20%) black (10%) To be replaced by the [Multitarn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multitarn) (6-Farben-Tarndruck) with the new equipment to be received by the German Army in 2028-29.[^cite_note-fa-118] |

| [Multitarn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multitarn) *6-Farben-Tarndruck* |  | [Germany](11867.md) | [Flecktarn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flecktarn) | Universal | Special forces camouflage in use in the [special forces of the German military](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kommando_Spezialkr%C3%A4fte).[^cite_note-zd-120] This pattern was developed with the aim to make it the new standard and universal pattern of the German Army., but has not yet entered service in the general troops.[^cite_note-zd-120] The pattern was developed by the Bundeswehr Research Institute for Materials and Operating Materials (WIWeB). It is a universal pattern that was unveiled in 2016. The aim and the colour scheme are comparable to the universal [MultiCam](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MultiCam) pattern. 6 colours are used.[^cite_note-121] In July 2025, the German military announced that the Multitarn would replace the [Flecktarn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flecktarn) (5-Farben-Tarndruck) in 2028-29 as the future standard camo.[^cite_note-fa-118] |
| Schneetarn |  | [Germany](11867.md) | [Flecktarn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flecktarn) | Winter / snow camo | Used by forces trained for these conditions.[^cite_note-122] |

### Electronic equipment

The [Infanterist der Zukunft](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Infanterist_der_Zukunft) (IdZ) program aims to equip individual German infantry soldiers with an array of protective gear and small arms attachments, navigation, optics, optronics, and communications devices. These devices are networked to enable electronic (radio) communications between individual actors (soldiers, vehicles) and tactical units (platoons, companies) in the battlefield. Germany has ordered 353 platoon systems, meaning about 12000 individual sets, of IdZ-ES (ES Enhanced System) from Rheinmetall electronics.[^cite_note-140]

## Vehicles

The current inventory of armoured vehicles, both serviceable and active, is likely to increase significantly due to the ongoing war in Ukraine after Russia's invasion. This caused a rethink of [Germany](11867.md)'s defence doctrine, which includes increased defence spending of at least 2% of [GDP](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GDP) and an initial outlay of €100 billion on expanding, improving and updating defence equipment. The German government is to outline its defence doctrine in the coming months, but is likely to include formations of additional combat brigades as well as support elements.[^cite_note-197][^cite_note-198][^cite_note-199]

The *Terrestrial Transmission System* is a long range communication tool. It enables communication between the network nodes of the Bundeswehr. Key element in a [network-centric warfare](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Network-centric_warfare) modern doctrine. 2 variants: on a trailer on a 6×6 MAN gl truck Accompanied by a communication truck module. |

Communications hub and universal voice and IP router that interoperably connects transmission networks and devices from different manufacturers and generations. Equipment TAN-T230A (interface for 6 analogue radio devices, 10 digital devices/networks)[^cite_note-463] 4 telescopic masts for antennas with pre-wired connections Integrated generator for independent operation A/C |
| RS NServTrp *Rüstsätzen Netzservicetrupp* | – | [Germany](11867.md)  [Switzerland](26748.md) | Container with network cable and connection kits | 43 (+76 on order; + 274 in option) | Supplied by Leonardo Deutschland and Solifos. Orders: 43 RS NServTrp in 2023 for the German VJTF[^cite_note-464][^cite_note-465] Budget approval for framework over 7 years for up to 350 RS NServTrp in December 2024 (€113.2 million).[^cite_note-ez-466] Firm orders: 76 in March 2025 for €22.8 million.[^cite_note-ez-466] |

| [FN M3M Mk3](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/M2_Browning) |  | [Belgium](3343.md) | [Side-door machine gun](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Door_gunner) ([12.7×99mm NATO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/12.7%C3%9799mm_NATO)) | [NH90 TTH](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NHIndustries_NH90) | 50 | [^cite_note-\:0-574] 50 machine guns ordered by the Bundeswehr for the NH90 in 2020, delivered in 2021-22.[^cite_note-575] |
